# 20 Content catalog

As of 2026-10-12. Status: EXTRACTED. Generated by `tools/catalog/gen_catalog.py` from `palimpsest/content/`, which `tools/catalog/extract_content.py` fills from the chapter tables. Do not hand-edit.

## Imports

| Source | Section | What this chapter consumes |
|---|---|---|
| Canon | §19 | Namespace ownership (generated into `meta.` records) |
| Canon | §17.7 | Retired IDs |
| Doc 19 | §1 to §7 | Content file layout, envelope and record schema pattern |
| Chapters 00 to 18 | Every table whose first cell is a backticked ID | The record source |

## 1. Coverage

Game records: 3439 from 19 owner chapters. Namespace prefixes: 119. Retired IDs: 19. Content files: 115.

Each record keeps its table row as text in `fields`. Field values are not checked against per-kind schemas yet; the schema per owner only checks the envelope and the owner.
